I'll list the cycles and compute n14 directly from the formula: n14 = NAND(q4, n4) = NAND(q4, NAND(q7, q2)). So n14 = NOT (q4 AND NOT (q7 AND q2)) = NOT q4 OR (q7 AND q2).
